# C · part 3

1,399 plugins, claude-guardian to cloud-provider-skills. [Back to the index](../README.md)

- **claude-guardian**: Self-learning convention enforcer — watches project patterns, auto-generates .claude/rules files, prevents architectural drift. Zero-config, learns over time. ([NodeNestor/claude-guardian](https://github.com/NodeNestor/claude-guardian))
- **claude-guardrails**: Thirteen PreToolUse hooks that block the specific things a coding agent gets wrong in a real codebase. ([RabihJabr29/claude-guardrails](https://github.com/RabihJabr29/claude-guardrails))
- **claude-guards**: Two hooks that block the two mistakes that cost the most: swallowing a huge file into context, and letting a shell silently corrupt a text file. ([xniperbuilds/claude-guards](https://github.com/xniperbuilds/claude-guards/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude-guards))
- **claude-guide**: Navigate Claude Code documentation and get quick answers about configuration, MCP servers, plugins, context optimization, session management, and best practices. ([source](github:whitebob/claude-hook-copilot@plugin))

- **claude-legal-federal-laws**: Shared federal reference plugin: canonical FDCPA / FCRA / TILA / ECOA / Reg B-Z + RESPA / SCRA / FHA / FTC Telemarketing Sales Rule, the Title 11 Bankruptcy Code, the model UCC, and the Americans with Disabilities Act with its DOJ/EEOC implementing regulations (42 U.S.C. ch. 126; 29 CFR 1630; 28 CFR 35/36 incl. the 2010 ADA Standards) — a dependency of every state plugin, dereferenced from in-tree symlinks at install time; plus a nationwide FCRA consumer credit-report-rights skills layer and an ADA self-help skill (ada-rights), two bundled free MCP servers (CourtListener + Legal Data Hunter),… ([codearranger/claude-legal](https://github.com/codearranger/claude-legal/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude-legal-federal-laws))
